**2026-08-04 — Admin UI cleanup, requested directly by the operator: the config page and the admin top nav had both grown past what a phone screen (or a scanning eye) can hold.**

- **The config page's `<h2>`/`<h3>` headers were already a grouping — nobody had ever made the page act on it.** `configSections()` (`src/render/admin.ts`) turns each existing header into a named group (Ageing, Care, Conformation, Conformation labels, Consignment dealer, Health, Incidents, Market, Miscellaneous, Money, Shows, Turns and events) with its fields and explanatory paragraphs moved in verbatim — no field renamed, no validation touched, no config key added or removed. The seven fields that had never had a header (`display_timezone`, `game_days_per_tick`, `game_days_per_year`, `max_stables_per_account`, `starting_stable_capacity`, `starting_balance`, `min_password_length`) became "Miscellaneous", per the operator's own instruction for exactly this case.
- **A plain GET picker, sorted alphabetically, shows one group at a time** (`<select name="section">` + a "Show" button, the same no-JavaScript pattern `market.ts`'s `breedPicker` already established, credited there in its own comment). Each group renders as its own `<form method="post">` carrying a hidden `section` field and only that group's inputs. That is safe without any change to `adminConfigRoute`'s save logic: it already does `if (rawValue === undefined) continue` per key, so a form missing every other section's fields simply leaves those config values untouched — the picker works *because* that endpoint was already partial-submission-safe, not because anything was added to make it so. The section survives both a successful save (the redirect now carries `?section=`) and a validation error (the error re-render does too), so retuning stays on the group you were looking at.
- **The admin top nav's flat row of eighteen links became six**: `Admin home` and `World clock` standalone (per the operator, both wanted "first" — home is the literal root of `/admin`, so it renders first, world clock right after), and four dropdown hubs — Game horses (Search horses, Founding stock, Ageing, Care, Health, Incidents), Players and NPCs (Accounts, Money, NPC stables, Consignment dealer), Behind the scenes (Breeds, Breeding, Shows), Settings (Config, Security, Migrations, Start over). The operator's own two example lists both named "Health" and neither named "Incidents"; asked directly, the operator put both in Game horses over Settings, reasoning they're about what's happening to a horse rather than the game's tuning knobs.
- **The dropdown is a `<details>`/`<summary>` per hub — no JavaScript, the identical mechanism `section-collapse` already uses** for the horse page's collapsed cards. `layout.ts` gained `NavHub` (`{ label, children: NavLink[], active }`) alongside the existing `NavLink`, and a `SubnavItem` union; `subnavBar()` now renders either kind, so the player-facing `stableSubnav` (Overview/Horses/Breed/Money/Change prefix, flat, five or six links, never wrapped in a hub) needed no change at all — passing a `NavLink[]` still works because a `NavLink[]` is a `SubnavItem[]`.
- **`.subnav` switched from `overflow-x: auto` + horizontal scroll to `flex-wrap: wrap`.** The scroll existed to cope with eighteen links that no longer fit a phone width; it also happened to violate the stylesheet's own header comment ("no horizontal scrolling"). It had to go regardless of the wrap, though — a `<details>` dropdown positioned `absolute` inside an `overflow: auto` ancestor gets clipped by that ancestor's scroll box, which would have made every hub menu invisible or scroll-trapped. `.subnav-hub`/`.subnav-hub-menu` are new; the dropdown reuses the existing `section-collapse` marker-removal trick (`list-style: none` + `::-webkit-details-marker { display: none }`) rather than inventing a second one.
- **Local dev needs a `.dev.vars` with `SESSION_SECRET` set that this repo does not ship** — `wrangler dev --local` throws inside `crypto.subtle.importKey` on login/setup without one (an empty HMAC key). Not new to this change, just newly hit while checking it in a browser; already covered by `.gitignore`, worth knowing before the next session reaches for `npm run dev` and gets a 500 with no obvious cause.

---

**2026-08-05 — Built `docs/fixes/breed-disease-panels.md`: breed-specific disease loci for six breeds, and the panel filter that makes breed matter for testing at all.**

- **The report.** An operator screenshot of `/admin/horses/new` plus two sentences: only the original four disease loci were on the create-a-horse form, and testing offered the same set for every breed. The fix document (already built by a prior session) diagnosed the real defect underneath both symptoms: `conditions.breed_associations` has been display-only since migration `0052`'s own comment said so, so every "what can I test for" screen showed the whole enabled list regardless of what a horse's breed could actually carry. A Friesian was offered five Quarter Horse tests it was genetically incapable of failing.
- **Seven new loci, migration `0145`**: SCID, CA and LFS (Arabian), WFFS (German Warmblood), DWARF and HYDRO (Friesian) — all from `docs/breed-disease-panels.md`'s own draft, teaching and event text reused verbatim — plus DSLD (Paso Fino), new to the fix document because the design document left Paso Fino with no buildable condition at all and the fix closes that gap with an honest disclosure ("nobody has yet found the single gene behind DSLD in real horses... this game gives it one gene so it can be tested"). MCOA (Icelandic) needed no new locus — it reads the existing Silver locus (`Z`) homozygous, the same trick lethal white overo already uses on frame overo.
- **`panelFor` (`src/engines/health/panel.ts`), one pure function, no database access**: a condition belongs on a horse's panel when any breed in its pedigree carries the mutant allele at nonzero frequency in `founding_allele_pool` — derived from the pools themselves, never from `breed_associations`, because a hand-maintained breed list can silently drift from the pools that actually decide what a horse can carry (the fix document's own "LWO trap": today `breed_associations = '["QH"]'` is correct only because the pool happens to say so, and a future pool edit would desynchronize the two with nothing to catch it). `pedigreeBreedCodes` (`src/db/health.ts`) resolves the breed-code set from `horse_ancestors`, not just `breed_id` — a Quarter Horse x Arabian foal can carry SCID, and filtering on registry breed alone would leave that risk with no test to buy.
- **Wired into the seven offer-and-disclosure call sites the fix document named**, and nowhere else: the test page (`buildTestPageRows`), the barn list's free-observation badge stays unfiltered on purpose (see below), the owner/admin Health card (`healthRowsFor`), a market listing's disclosure (`disclosedConditionsFor`), the breeding preview's health warning (`breedingHealthWarningsFor`, now the *union* of both parents' panels — a cross-breed pairing warns on both sides), an NPC stable's own pre-listing panel purchase (`npcMarket.ts`), and the consignment dealer's pre-test (`consignment.ts`, filtered per-candidate to that candidate's own single breed, since founding stock has no pedigree). **Truth stays unfiltered, deliberately**: `buildHorseConditionStatements`, `killDueLethalFoals`, `isBarredFromShowing`, `visibleAffectedConditions`, `getLethalTriggers` and `conditionCensus` all read a genotype directly and never consult a panel — a horse's status has never depended on what is worth testing for, and a test (`test/health/pedigree-panel-d1.test.ts`) pins a hand-built double-GBED Friesian genotype still reading `affected` to stop a future session "tidying" the filter into the engines.
- **PSSM1 widened to German Warmblood and Paso Fino** (migration `0146`, same frequency the Quarter Horse already carries) — real, not Quarter-Horse-exclusive, biology, and what gives those two breeds a second panel condition apiece with no invented locus. This immediately desynchronized PSSM1's own `breed_associations` (still `'["QH"]'` from the already-applied `0053`), caught by a new test (`test/genetics/consistency.test.ts`) that checks every condition's `breed_associations` against the pools directly rather than trusting either side — fixed with a follow-up migration (`0152`) rather than editing `0053`, per CLAUDE.md §8's forward-only rule.
- **Hydrocephalus is seeded `degenerative`, not `lethal`** — an operator decision recorded in the fix document, overriding the design document's own §5.6. Seeding it lethal as drafted would have put the game's lethal count at six (GBED, LWO, SCID, LFS, WFFS, hydrocephalus), over overview §3b's own four-or-five ceiling; the design document's count of five predates LWO, which landed later in slice 0021. `terminal_game_day` stays NULL and `state` is `'onset'`, which falls out of `buildHorseConditionStatements`'s existing `severity_class` branch with no special case.
- **Signs now take time to show.** Before this, `signs_visible = 1` meant visible the instant a `horse_conditions` row was written — a newborn foal was flagged with HERDA on its birthday, and a horse bought at four came with HYPP already known before anyone had worked it. `conditions` gained `signs_delay_min_game_days`/`signs_delay_max_game_days` (migrations `0148`-`0149`): 100-250 game days as the default band for HYPP/PSSM1/HERDA, 20-60 for CA (real onset is weeks, not months), 250-400 for DSLD (adult onset by nature), 0 for everything genuinely visible at birth (DWARF, HYDRO, LFS, WFFS, MCOA — unchanged from today). `horse_conditions` gained `signs_game_day` (migration `0150`, drawn once per horse from a sub-seed off its own `rng_seed` via `deriveSeed`/`makeRng` — never `Math.random()`, and reproducible, pinned by `test/health/signs-delay.test.ts`) and `signs_noticed_game_day`, the idempotency marker a new tick stage, `noticeDueConditionSigns` (sitting right after `killDueLethalFoals`), sets when it writes the delayed `condition_signs` event. `buildHorseConditionStatements` no longer writes that event itself — `buildConditionSignsEventStatement` (`src/db/events.ts`) is dead and removed. `isBarredFromShowing` and `visibleAffectedConditions` both gained a `gameDay` parameter and now follow the signs, not the raw genotype - `isBarredFromShowing` also gained a `horseId` parameter, since it now has to look up that horse's own `signs_game_day`. Pre-existing `horse_conditions` rows get no backfill (`signs_game_day` stays NULL for them, same precedent migration `0050`'s own header sets for its four loci) and both functions treat a NULL or missing entry as **already due** - a horse already showing signs today keeps showing them, and nothing is ever let into a show by a lookup gap.
- **The admin create-horse form's loci list — nineteen fieldsets became twenty-six — is now grouped into collapsed `<details>` sections by category** (Base, Dilutions, Patterns, Gait, Disease), the same treatment slice 0021 Part F already gave the test page's own colour panel. Left deliberately unfiltered by breed (a neutral control, slice 0005 §6.6 - the operator must be able to build a horse the generator never would), with one muted line explaining that an allele set outside its own breed will be real but untestable.
- **Two wording fixes found while wiring this in.** The test page's "Five-panel genotype test" button description was hardcoded for the Quarter Horse's own count; it now reads the actual number bought (`${toBuy.length}-condition panel test`), since an Arabian's panel is three and a Thoroughbred's is none. And `src/engines/showing/eligibility.ts`'s own comments still said `horse_conditions.outcome` for the acquired-incident flags two full lines after slice 0022 Part A moved that truth to `horse_incidents` - comment-only, but in this codebase the comments are the handoff.
- **One more forward-only migration (`0151`)**: drops `horse_conditions.resolve_game_day`/`treated_game_day`/`outcome`, three columns migration `0122` added for the acquired-incident lifecycle that slice 0022 Part A's move to `horse_incidents` (and `0133`'s delete of every acquired row) left written by nothing and read by nothing - exactly the "nullable column nothing touches is a promise nobody keeps" CLAUDE.md warns against.
- Six new test files/additions: `test/health/panel.test.ts` (`panelFor` in isolation), `test/health/pedigree-panel-d1.test.ts` (the ancestry rule and the truth-is-unfiltered guard, against a real `horse_ancestors` table), `test/health/signs-delay.test.ts` (the delay draw, its bounds, its reproducibility, and `visibleAffectedConditions`'s due-gating), plus updates to `test/genetics/consistency.test.ts` (the new loci/pools, and the new `breed_associations`-vs-pools cross-check), `test/founding/generate.test.ts`/`test/health/pools.test.ts` (their own pool-reading migration name bumped from `0114` to `0146`, and the PSSM1 widening asserted rather than contradicted).
- Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm test` green (83 files, 783 tests, up from 748 before this session's new tests — the consistency test's own `breed_associations` cross-check caught the PSSM1 staleness above before it shipped). **Not** verified in a browser or against real D1 - nobody has looked at the grouped admin create-horse form or the test page's new ancestry sentence on a real screen, and the world has not been reset to pick up the new loci on existing stock (the same "Part G" step the colour-loci slice already left for the operator).
- Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm test` green (76 files, 697 tests, unchanged from before this change — no test touches admin rendering, and the one flaky CPU-timing test in `test/genetics/foal-colours.test.ts` noted in earlier entries did not reproduce this run). **Verified in a browser**, both changes: `wrangler dev --local` against a freshly migrated local D1, screenshotted at desktop and 390px-wide mobile viewports — the config picker's section switch and per-section save round-trip both confirmed live (saved one section's value, confirmed an unrelated section's value was untouched), and the nav hubs confirmed to open, highlight the active hub/link correctly, and wrap without horizontal scroll on mobile.

**2026-08-05 — Slice 0025 stage 4: Novice/Open/Champion progression and on-demand class creation.** Closes out `docs/slices/0025-difficulty-foals-shows-and-evaluation.md`, the last of its four stages. Two operator decisions taken before building, both recorded in the slice document itself (§7.5a.1): keep `show_entry_window_game_days` at 30 rather than shortening it ("30 game days. that's one real day."), and young-horse classes (`young_conformation`/`ability_test`) stay single-rank, matching §7.3's own ribbons-don't-count-toward-adult-progression rule.

- **Classes are minted on demand now, not on a fixed calendar.** `createDueShows` is unchanged in what it does, but it now checks a new flag, `flags.show_auto_create_enabled` (migration `0167`, seeded `false`) - the calendar path stays in the codebase, unused, as the operator's own named "way back" if an empty `/shows` ever reads as broken rather than quiet. In its place, `requestClassEntry` (`src/db/shows.ts`) is what the horse page's "Enter in a show" button now calls: it joins a live class matching the request if one is open, or mints exactly one otherwise, via `mintClassForSpec`/`getOrCreateShowForDay`. The horse page itself no longer lists open classes - `buildCatalogueStatusForHorse` builds the **whole catalogue** (`buildShowCatalogue`: every breed's conformation class, every enabled discipline, both young-horse bands' conformation and ability-test classes) and answers eligibility for each row whether or not a live class currently exists for it, so a class that has never been entered still shows a real "Enter" button rather than being invisible.
- **The partial-unique-index idempotency guarantee the slice document specified is built as written, with one addition it didn't anticipate.** `show_classes` gained `class_key` (migration `0165`, a rebuild for the same SQLite-can't-ALTER-a-CHECK reason migrations `0064`/`0161` needed) - a single NOT NULL column folding `(class_type, breed_id, discipline_code, ability_trait_code, age_band)` down to one string (`classKeyFor`, e.g. `bc:1`, `disc:barrel_racing`, `yc:1:yearling`), because **SQLite treats every NULL as distinct from every other NULL in a unique index**, and all four class types leave at least one of those columns NULL on every row - the literal multi-column index the slice proposed would never actually collide. `idx_show_classes_open_key` is `UNIQUE(class_key, rank) WHERE status = 'scheduled'`.
- **Rank is a real column, not a derived fact.** `show_classes.rank` is `'novice'|'open'|'champion'` for a breed_conformation/discipline class, or the fixed sentinel `'none'` for young_conformation/ability_test (never rank-gated, enforced by the same CHECK the class-type pairing already used). `horse_class_ranks` (migration `0166`) is the permanent per-horse, per-class-type record - one row per `(horse_id, class_key)`, using the same `bc:`/`disc:` prefixes so the two tables' keys are directly comparable.
- **Graduation is the operator's own compound rule, implemented literally rather than as a fuzzy points system.** "At least four ribbons 1st-3rd, including at least one 1st" became two config keys (`show_rank_top3_required` = 4, `show_rank_win_required` = 1, migration `0167`) and two counters (`top3_since_promotion`/`wins_since_promotion`) that reset to 0 on every promotion - a fresh qualifying run is needed for each rank, the same way a real breed association's point system works, not a lifetime bank. The slice document offered "points" as the implementation to aim at and the compound rule as an equivalent alternative; the literal rule was chosen because it needs no invented per-placing point values and is exactly what a family member would describe out loud. `nextRankProgressionRow`/`buildRankProgressionUpsertStatement` (`src/db/shows.ts`) run for every placed entry in a judged breed_conformation/discipline class, player and NPC alike - a horse already at `champion` still has its counters updated (visible at `/admin`) but `RANK_ORDER` has nothing past it to promote to.
- **A departure from a literal reading of §7.5a, recorded here per CLAUDE.md §2.** The slice's own text says a request "mints only when there is none, or when this stable already holds its `max_entries_per_stable` in the live one" - read literally, a stable hitting its own cap would get a *second*, concurrently-open class of the identical `(class_key, rank)`. That directly undoes the same section's stated reason for preferring join-over-mint in the first place ("three children each asking for a dressage show on different days would otherwise get three shows of one entry apiece... the sibling rivalry quietly disappears") - a fourth same-stable horse hitting the cap would fragment the field exactly that way. Built instead: hitting the per-stable cap on the live class refuses with the existing `entry_cap_reached` reason rather than minting a parallel class. `test/showing/rank-and-on-demand-d1.test.ts` pins this choice by name.
- **§7.5a.1's required fix (independent of stage 4, but rebuilt in passing here since stage 4 replaces the same function) is built.** The horse page's "Enter in a show" card used to call `checkHorseEligibilityForClass` once per open class - roughly 160 D1 round trips at stage 3's 40-class catalogue, on the screen the children use most. `buildCatalogueStatusForHorse` hoists every per-horse fact (genotype, phenotype, `isBarredFromShowing`, incident flags, availability) out of the per-row loop and computes it once, then answers every catalogue row from a handful of batched queries (every live class in one query, this horse's open entries in one, this stable's per-class counts in one, every rank this horse has ever earned in one) - a small constant number of round trips regardless of catalogue size. The NPC top-up loop inside `judgeOneClass` got the identical treatment on its own axis (one class, many candidate horses): `eligibleNpcHorsesForClass` replaces a per-horse `checkHorseEligibilityForClass` call with `isBarredFromShowingMap`/`acquiredBarringFlagsMap` (both new batched siblings of the existing single-horse functions in `src/db/health.ts`/`src/db/incidents.ts`, which now delegate to their own map versions rather than duplicating the query) plus one grouped rank query and one grouped stable-count query for the whole candidate pool. Rank-gating an NPC horse falls out of this for free, closing the operator's own "let the npcs compete like real players" instruction.
- **`/shows` no longer assumes there is only ever one open show.** `getNextShow` (earliest open show, singular) is replaced by `listOpenShows` - on demand, a barrel-racing show and a dressage show can genuinely be open at once, which the calendar system's own interval-matching entry window made moot before. The index page groups by open show as before, just over a list instead of a single optional card, and now reads "Open for entries" rather than "Next up" since there may be several or none.
- **`engines/showing/eligibility.ts` gained one more reason, `wrong_rank`**, and every `EligibilityHorse`/`EligibilityClass` now carries a `rank` field (`ShowRank`/`ClassRank`). The horse page's own "Enter" button can never produce this refusal - it always targets the horse's own current rank via `requestClassEntry` - so it is only reachable from `/shows/:id`'s browse-any-open-class form, which still lists a class by id and would otherwise let an unranked horse into a field it never earned a place in.
- **A sixteenth table referencing `horses`.** `horse_class_ranks` joins `horse_ability_words` in both `src/db/reset.ts` (a real foreign key, must be cleared before `horses` in either reset scope) and `src/db/horseRemoval.ts`'s two lists (a DELETE in `buildDeleteHorseStatements`, never a `deletableHorseSql` keep-clause - in practice a horse with a rank row always also has a `horse_show_summary` row, which already blocks the delete, so this always removes zero rows in the cases that reach it, same as `horse_ability_words` before it). `test/reset.test.ts`'s hand-maintained dependency map and `test/reset-d1.test.ts`'s schema-driven cross-check both updated/pass.
- **`onDemandCalendarEntryFor`** (`src/engines/showing/calendar.ts`) is a cosmetic-only sibling of `calendarEntryFor` for a show whose `scheduled_game_day` is `gameDay + window` rather than an exact multiple of `show_interval_game_days` - the existing function's own documented precondition. Purely a venue/name generator; nothing reads it for a rule.
- New test file `test/showing/rank-and-on-demand-d1.test.ts` (7 tests, same `node:sqlite` harness as `young-and-ability-classes-d1.test.ts`, excluded from `tsc` in `tsconfig.json` for the same `node:fs`/`node:sqlite` reason): promotion after 4 top-3s including a win with counters reset, champion never promoting further while still accumulating, a partial run keeping its running counts, mint-then-join on repeated requests for the same catalogue row, the per-stable-cap departure above, an Open-ranked and a Novice-ranked horse landing in two different classes for the same catalogue row, and a too-young horse refused with no class ever minted. `test/showing/eligibility.test.ts` gained two unit cases for `wrong_rank` directly. `test/showing/young-and-ability-classes-d1.test.ts`'s own `readConfig` now opts back into `show_auto_create_enabled` explicitly, since it exercises `createDueShows` directly and the flag now defaults off.
- Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm test` green (90 files, 850 tests, up from 843 before this session's new tests). **Not** verified in a browser - the new catalogue card on the horse page and the multi-show `/shows` index have not been looked at live, and no local D1 has been migrated against the two rebuild migrations (`0165`/`0161`-shaped) outside the test harness.

**2026-08-05 — A second German Warmblood discipline barn, Dressur Stables, targeting Dressage.**

- Requested directly (an NPC stable that breeds for show dressage horses, German Warmblood). Rather than repointing the breed's existing discipline barn (Springen Stables, jumping) - which a family may already be competing against and shouldn't have its target silently swapped - this adds an eleventh NPC personality stable, `Dressur Stables` (migration `0168`, pure data, same shape as migration `0137`), a `discipline_barn` policy targeting `dressage` with the exact `DISCIPLINE_BARN_NUMBERS` every other discipline barn uses. Named to match `GW_DISCIPLINE_BARN_PREFIX`'s own precedent of a German discipline word ("Springen" for jumping; "Dressur" is German for dressage) rather than the family's own naming, since no operator naming session produced this one - flagged here in case the operator wants to rename it from `/admin/npc`.
- `GW_DRESSAGE_BARN_PREFIX` added to `src/db/npc.ts` beside the other named prefixes, and `src/db/reset.ts`'s full-world-reset re-seed gained the matching `personalityStableStatements` call, with its own header comment's stable/policy counts bumped ten→eleven and eight→nine.
- **Every test file that hardcodes a player stable id had to move up by one** - `PLAYER`/`SELLER`/`BUYER`/`MARE_STABLE`/`STALLION_STABLE`/`STABLE`/`PLAYER_STABLE` across nine files, all following the same "migrations seed N NPC stables, so a player stable starts at N+1" convention `test/npc/buying-d1.test.ts` established. A future session adding another seeded NPC stable will hit the same set and should grep for that convention's comment rather than rediscovering it test-by-test.
- Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm test` green (90 files, 850 tests, unchanged - the shift only renumbered existing fixtures). **Not** verified in a browser or against real D1.

**Also verified, not built: whether the just-shipped Novice/Open/Champion progression (the entry directly above this one) counts ribbons a horse won before it existed.** It does not. `migrations/0166_horse_class_ranks.sql` creates `horse_class_ranks` empty, with no `INSERT ... SELECT` from `show_entries`, and `nextRankProgressionRow`/`horseRankForClass` (`src/db/shows.ts`) only ever read that table - a horse with no row reads `'novice'` with both counters at 0 regardless of how many 1st-3rd placings its `show_entries` history already holds from the flat, unranked system that existed until today. This was not a decision the slice document made explicitly either way (`docs/slices/0025-difficulty-foals-shows-and-evaluation.md` §7.5/§7.5a describe the graduation rule and the migration's own comment on `show_classes.rank` only reasons about **classes** never having had a rank before, not about a horse's own prior wins) - it is an open question this session is flagging rather than silently resolving, per CLAUDE.md §2. No code changed for this.

**2026-08-05 — Backfilled the Novice/Open/Champion rank progression from show history, and confirmed progression is scoped per class_key (per breed, per discipline), not per horse overall.**

- **Verified first, per the operator's own follow-up question.** Confirmed by reading `classKeyFor` (`src/db/shows.ts`): a `discipline` class's key is `disc:<discipline_code>` and `horse_class_ranks` is `UNIQUE(horse_id, class_key)`, so a German Warmblood's dressage rank and jumping rank are two entirely separate rows - four dressage ribbons promote it to Open in dressage while it can sit at Novice in jumping with zero ribbons there. This was already true by construction (the table's own design), not something this session needed to build - locked in with a new test, `'tracks progression independently per discipline'` in `test/showing/rank-and-on-demand-d1.test.ts`, using a `seedSoloDisciplineRound` helper alongside the existing breed_conformation-only `seedSoloRound`.
- **The backfill itself: `backfillHistoricalRanks` (`src/db/shows.ts`), triggered from a new "Recompute rank progress" button on `/admin/shows`** (`POST` action `backfill_ranks`, `src/routes/admin.ts`/`src/render/admin.ts` - no confirm checkbox, since the operation only ever recomputes a derived value and is safe to press more than once). Reads every judged `show_entries` row in a rank-tracked class (`breed_conformation`/`discipline`) joined to its `show_classes` row, groups by `(horse_id, class_key)`, and folds each horse's own placings **in the order they were actually judged** through the exact same `nextRankProgressionRow` the live judging path (`judgeOneClass`) already uses - no second implementation of the graduation rule to keep in step with the first.
- **Deliberately recomputes every pair from scratch and overwrites, rather than skipping a pair that already has a row.** The obvious-looking alternative - skip any `(horse_id, class_key)` the live system has already written - has a real bug: this session runs hours after the progression itself shipped, on the same calendar day, so any horse whose *first* live-judged placing landed today (before this backfill ran) would already have a row seeded from only today's placing, and skipping it would permanently exclude every ribbon it won before today. Recomputing unconditionally from the complete `show_entries` history is deterministic and idempotent - given the same rows, it always converges on exactly the rank/counters live judging would have produced had the progression existed from day one, whether run once or ten times. Four new tests in `test/showing/rank-and-on-demand-d1.test.ts` pin this: a straight reconstruction matches what live judging actually produced (built via the same `seedSoloRound`/`judgeDueShowClasses` path, then `DELETE FROM horse_class_ranks` to simulate the pre-tracking world before comparing), the same for two independent disciplines, a second press reaching the identical answer, and a no-op when there's no rank-tracked history at all.
- No migration needed - `horse_class_ranks` already exists (migration `0166`); this is a data operation, not a schema change, so it lives in `src/db/shows.ts` and an admin action rather than a `/migrations` file, the same reasoning `src/db/ageing.ts`'s `backfillMissingLifespans` already established for a derived-value backfill that's more than a straight `UPDATE`.
- Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm test` green (90 files, 855 tests, up from 850). **Not** verified in a browser - the new button on `/admin/shows` has not been clicked against real D1, and no session has real historical ribbons to backfill yet (this is a fresh addition alongside the progression system itself).

**2026-08-05 — Slice 0026 stage 1: gelding.** `docs/slices/0026-gelding-horse-page-tabs-ranks-and-the-show-barn.md`, first of its four independent stages. Only stage 1 is built this session; stages 2-4 (horse-page tabs, rank display, the show barn) are untouched.

- **`POST /horses/:id/geld`, GET-confirmed exactly like `/retire`/`/pet-home`** (`renderGeldConfirmPage`, `src/render/horses.ts`; `horseGeldRoute`, `src/routes/horses.ts`). Costs `gelding_cost` (400, first guess) and one turn - a real decision, unlike retiring away or the pet home, which cost neither. Only ever reachable for a living stallion owned by the acting stable; anything else 404s the same way `horseStudRoute` already 404s a non-stallion.
- **Two different kinds of "no", and they render differently.** A hard-fact refusal (not owner, not alive, not a stallion) 404s, same as every other horse-scoped route. A *temporary* one - unavailable (out at pasture / still settling in) or an unresolved paid stud booking standing against him - is computed once (`computeBlocked`) and shown as a sentence on the confirmation page itself, with no submit button drawn, rather than only discovered after the child presses Confirm. This is the same "don't invite a click that was always going to fail" rule `docs/fixes/breeding-eligibility-display.md` established for the breeding pickers, applied to a new action. `unresolvedStudBookingForStallion` (`src/db/stud.ts`) is a new query joining `stud_bookings` → `coverings` → `horses` for a `status = 'booked'` row against this stallion, returning the mare's own display fields so the refusal sentence can name her with the existing `displayNameFor` unchanged (it already accepts exactly that `Pick<HorseRow, 'registered_name'|'barn_name'|'sex'>` shape).
- **What gelding actually writes, in one batch:** `buildGeldStatements` (`src/db/ageing.ts`) - `horses.sex = 'gelding'`, `horses.gelded_game_day` (migration `0169`, nullable `INTEGER`) - plus his own unresolved coverings cancelled with a new reason, `sire_gelded`. Kept as its own exported constant (`SIRE_GELDED_REASON`) rather than widening `cancelledReasonFor`, whose signature is keyed on `('dead' | 'removed')` and a gelding is neither. Only the `stallion_id` half of the WHERE clause is needed - unlike `buildEndHorseParticipationStatements`' own covering cancel, a gelding was never a mare. Pulled out of the route into its own testable builder (mirroring `buildEndHorseParticipationStatements`'s own reason for existing) specifically so it could be checked against a real database rather than only by hand - `test/ageing/geld-d1.test.ts` (new `node:sqlite` harness, same shape `test/market/stud-d1.test.ts` established) confirms an unresolved covering is cancelled `sire_gelded` while an already-resolved one is untouched, and - the one the slice calls out explicitly (§1.2 "existing pregnancies stand") - a live `in_progress` pregnancy off that resolved covering is never touched by any of this. The rest of the batch reuses existing builders unchanged: `buildWithdrawStudListingsForHorseStatement` (his stud listing), `buildLedgerStatements` (a new `gelding` ledger kind, migration `0171` widening the CHECK the same way `0160_ledger_add_time_warp_kind.sql` did), and `buildEventStatement` (a new `gelded` event kind - `events.kind` has no CHECK, so no migration needed there, same as every event kind since slice 0010).
- **Recovery window.** A new `EligibilityReason`, `recovering_from_gelding`, true while `game_day - horses.gelded_game_day < gelding_recovery_game_days` (30, first guess) - computed by one pure helper, `isRecoveringFromGelding` (`src/engines/showing/eligibility.ts`), called identically from all three places that build an `EligibilityHorse` in `src/db/shows.ts` (`checkHorseEligibilityForClass`, `buildCatalogueStatusForHorse`, `eligibleNpcHorsesForClass`) so the three cannot drift on the window. Checked in `checkEligibility` right after the location/availability check and before every other rule, the same "a wait with an end date" footing `settling_in` already stands on (§1.3's own instruction).
- **The show bonus: a gelding's negative noise only is softened, mares and stallions untouched.** `geldingAdjustedNoise` (`src/engines/showing/noise.ts`) is a pure transform of the value `noiseForEntry` already drew - no new RNG stream, so a re-fired tick still reproduces the class byte for byte. Wired into `judgeOneClass` (`src/db/shows.ts`) in exactly one place, ahead of the `usesAbilityScoring` branch, so it reaches all four class types (conformation, discipline, young-horse, ability) with no per-scorer special case. `show_gelding_noise_relief` (0.75, first guess) is a **live** config value, deliberately not snapshotted onto the class the way `noise_sd` beside it is - following the `discipline_aptitudes` precedent (slice 0022) rather than the class-snapshot rule slice 0008 set for everything else about a class's own scoring inputs. Because it's live, `score_breakdown` grows a `gelding` block (raw noise, the relief factor applied, the adjusted noise) as the only surviving record of what a horse was actually judged with, the same reasoning the `aptitude` block already carries for the same reason.
- **Appraisal: two new multiplicative terms, both modest, both compounding on purpose.** `market_gelding_factor` (0.75) applies when `sex = 'gelding'`. `market_rank_factors` (`{"novice":1.0,"open":1.15,"champion":1.35}`, a JSON map) is keyed by the horse's own **highest** rank held across every `horse_class_ranks` row it holds - a horse with none reads `novice`. `highestRankHeld` (`src/engines/showing/eligibility.ts`) is the one place this comparison is made, so `appraise()` today and the barn-list badge stage 3 will add later can't drift on which rank counts as "higher". Both new `AppraiseParams` fields (`isGelding`, `highestRank`) are threaded through the single database-side builder, `appraiseHorseForStable` (`src/db/listings.ts`) - every other caller (the market, buy offers, the NPC purchase path, the horse page's own guide value) goes through that one function, so nothing else needed a separate change. `test/market/appraise.test.ts`'s own structural "every AppraiseParams key, nothing about the lifespan" test (§14.2) had its expected key list extended rather than weakened - the point of that test is that adding a field is visible, not blocked.
- **One naming choice worth flagging per CLAUDE.md §2:** the slice's own prose names `withdrawStudListing` (the single-listing, form-driven function `/market/stud`'s own withdraw button calls) for the stud-listing withdrawal step, but this route needs it inside the same atomic batch as the rest of gelding's writes - `buildWithdrawStudListingsForHorseStatement` (the batch-shaped sibling `horseRetireRoute`/`horsePetHomeRoute` already use for the identical reason) was used instead. Same effect, correct atomicity; flagged in case the prose reference was meant to signal something else.
- Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm test` green (91 files, 865 tests, up from 855 before this session's new tests). **Not** verified in a browser or against real D1 outside the `node:sqlite` test harness - nobody has pressed the Geld button on a live `wrangler dev`, and the show-noise relief has not been watched change an actual placing.

**2026-08-05 — Slice 0026 stage 2: the horse page's four tabs.** `docs/slices/0026-gelding-horse-page-tabs-ranks-and-the-show-barn.md` §2. Stages 3-4 (rank display, the show barn) are still untouched.

- **The problem, in the slice's own words: `/horses/:id` renders seventeen blocks.** They are now grouped behind four tabs - Genetics (Conformation + evaluation, Ability, Colour and gait, Pedigree, Genotype), Health & care (Health, Incidents, Care, Location), Shows (Show record + Enter a class), Buying & selling (Sell, Stud, gelding/pet home/retire, admin delete). **No JavaScript**: they are plain links carrying `?tab=`, exactly like the barn list's own tabs (slice 0016 §4.1), and reuse the same `.subnav`/`.subnav-link` CSS the barn list already puts inside a page body - no stylesheet change at all.
- **`src/lib/horseTab.ts` is the one home for the rule** - `HorseTab`, `HORSE_TABS` (order = draw order, first = default), `parseHorseTab` and `horsePageUrl`. Pure, no imports, testable without a database, following `src/lib/barnFilter.ts`'s precedent for the barn list's equivalent.
- **`horsePageUrl(horseId, tab, params)` is now the only way a route builds a link back to this page**, and that is the load-bearing part of §2.2: *every POST handler redirects back to its own tab*, so a success notice is never left sitting behind a button the player isn't looking at. A hand-built `` redirect(`/horses/${id}?care_done=...`) `` lands them on Genetics with their notice invisible, which from the outside is indistinguishable from the action having done nothing. About two dozen redirects across `src/routes/horses.ts` were converted; `test/horse-tabs-source.test.ts` reads that file off disk and fails if a new one is built by hand, the same way `test/router-paths.test.ts` reads `src/router.ts`.
- **Three blocks belong to no tab and are pinned above the bar** (§2.1): the vitals card, the Register-a-name and Barn-name forms, and the Grow up card. Their forms carry a hidden `tab` field naming whichever tab the player was on, read back by `tabFromForm` (`src/routes/horses.ts`), so saving a barn name from the Shows tab returns to the Shows tab rather than yanking the player to Genetics. Three redirects genuinely have no tab of their own - a booked covering (shows in the mare status line, in the vitals), and the two image-picker outcomes (the portrait is in the vitals too) - and those pass `DEFAULT_HORSE_TAB` explicitly rather than being left hand-built, so the source-reading test above stays strict.
- **A tab whose cards all render empty still draws its button**, with a muted line inside (`horseTabBody`, filtering on `SafeHtml.value.trim()`). The tab bar has to be the same four buttons for every viewer - the only non-owner who reaches this page is an admin (everybody else is redirected to `/world/horses/:id` by slice 0016 §6.5), and a bar that changed shape under them would read as a broken page.
- **`renderHorsePage` still receives every parameter it always did** - §2.3 is explicit that skipping the loads for hidden tabs is a separate change, worth real time but only once the tabs themselves are proven, since it means making ~60 render params optional.
- Two small changes that fell out of the move: the Pedigree tables are wrapped in a `card` like everything else on the page now that they sit inside a tab body rather than at the bottom, and gelding/pet-home/retire are gathered into one card headed **"Changes that can't be undone"** - all three are one-way, and a child weighing one should see the others beside it. The inbreeding notice lost the word "below" (it is above the tab bar now and can no longer point down the page at the measurements).
- **Not built, deliberately, because §2.2 says so:** `<details class="section-collapse">` is untouched everywhere it already appears (the colour groups, the evaluation block, the genotype table). Tabs group cards; collapses group sub-sections inside a card. The two do not merge.
- Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm test` green (93 files, 876 tests, up from 865). Also rendered a real horse page on all four tabs plus a nonsense `?tab=` through the existing `node:sqlite` harness and read the output - each tab draws only its own cards, the nonsense value falls back to Genetics, and the vitals/name/Grow-up blocks appear on all five. **Not** verified in a browser against real D1.

**2026-08-05 — Slice 0026 stage 4: the show barn.** `docs/slices/0026-gelding-horse-page-tabs-ranks-and-the-show-barn.md` §4. Stage 3 (rank display and the Enter card) was already built and merged before this session started but never got a build-log entry - CLAUDE.md §10's row still read "stages 1-2 built... stages 3-4 not started" despite `targetRank`, the ribbon rank-grouping, and the catalogue prune all being live in `src/db/shows.ts`/`src/render/shows.ts`. Both CLAUDE.md and this gap are fixed alongside stage 4 below.

- **The problem, in the slice's own words: Apples and Oats Ranch has been doing double duty since migration 0085** - the Quarter Horse volume breeder (an `npc_policy` row) *and* the show barn (`SHOW_BARN_PREFIX`, migration 0040's original role) are the same stable. Split: a new stable takes over the show-barn role, `SHOW_BARN_PREFIX` now points at it, and Apples and Oats Ranch keeps its `npc_policy` row and nothing else changes about it.
- **Naming departure from the slice, flagged per CLAUDE.md §2: the new stable is not named/prefixed `Fair Meadow`.** §4.2 calls that prefix "free again" now that migration 0136 renamed the original stable away from it - it is not. `stable_prefix_history.prefix` is `UNIQUE` with no scoping to "currently active" (that table's own migration-0007 comment: "once a stable renames, its old prefix disappears from `stables` but this table still remembers it was taken" - a retired prefix is *never* reissued, by design, so two horses decades apart never share a confusingly identical registered-name prefix). Migration 0136 already closed `'Fair Meadow'` there permanently; reusing it in migration 0172 failed outright against the real constraint (caught immediately by the existing `node:sqlite` test harness, not by inspection). Migration 0172 instead names/prefixes the new stable `Fair Meadow Show Barn` (both fields identical), the same shape migration 0168 used for Dressur Stables.
- **Stock is rank-seeded at mint, never earned.** `npc_show_barn_rank_plan` (config, a real array embedded with `json()` the same way `quality_bands`/`market_rank_factors` are - **not** a double-encoded string, a mistake this session made once and caught via a failing `JSON.parse` test) replaces the old flat `npc_show_barn_quality_band`: `[{"rank":"novice","count":2,"band":"mid"},{"rank":"open","count":4,"band":"mid"},{"rank":"champion","count":4,"band":"high"}]`, 10 per breed in play, up from 6. `topUpShowBarnToPlan` (`src/db/npc.ts`) tops up only the shortfall per `(breed, rank)`, run by a new tick stage right before `judgeDueShowClasses` (so a class judged this tick sees a barn already refilled) and by `/admin/shows`'s new "Restock show barn to plan" button, which replaces the old band-picker form entirely.
- **Rank seeding and the freeze that keeps it meaningful.** A show-barn horse is seeded a `horse_class_ranks` row at mint (`buildShowBarnRankSeedStatement`, using the same `(SELECT id FROM horses ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1)` trick `buildHorseConditionStatements` already uses, since both run inside the same per-horse batch) for its own breed's conformation key (`bc:<breedId>`) plus every *enabled* discipline where that breed's `discipline_aptitudes` reads above neutral (`showBarnRankSeedClassKeysForBreed`) - a German Warmblood champion gets three rows (`bc:GW`, `disc:jumping`, `disc:dressage`), a Paso Fino champion gets two. **It never earns another one**: `judgeOneClass`'s rank-progression block (slice 0025 stage 4's own code) now filters `allHorseIds` against the show barn's own stable id before writing any `buildRankProgressionUpsertStatement`, so a show-barn horse can place, win, and be judged over and over with its seeded row never moving - without this the Champion field would slowly fill with computer horses that climbed there by winning, which is the NPC quality ceiling (CLAUDE.md §13) arriving through a side door.
- **Death at decline start, and a wider age spread.** `mintFoundingHorses` (`src/db/npc.ts`) now detects "is this mint going into the show barn" by comparing the target stable's own prefix against `SHOW_BARN_PREFIX` - cheap, no extra query - and when true, fixes `natural_death_game_day = born_game_day + age_decline_start_game_days` instead of rolling a lifespan (the roll still happens, from the horse's own seed, so every other founding path's RNG stream is bit-for-bit unaffected by which value actually gets stored - `naturalDeathGameDayOverride`, a new optional field on `FoundingHorseInsertInput`), and draws its age from `npc_show_barn_age_min/max_game_days` (1440/5040 - 4 to 14 game years) rather than `founding_age_min/max_game_days` (1440/2880). A show-barn horse therefore competes at a 1.0 age modifier its whole life and dies the exact day it would first be penalised, with turnover spread continuously across a decade rather than arriving as a wave. Because this lives inside `mintFoundingHorses` rather than behind a caller-supplied flag, it reaches *any* mint into the show barn automatically, including a manual outcross from `/admin/npc` - the escape hatch just starts those horses at Novice, since only the plan-aware top-up knows what rank to seed.
- **The NPC top-up inside `judgeOneClass` is now genuinely two-tier**, replacing the old "every `is_npc` stable's stock, undifferentiated, shuffled" pool. Tier 1 is every stable with an `npc_policy` row (a breeding stable), with stables scoped to the exact class (matching `target_breed_id`/`target_discipline_code`) shuffled ahead of unscoped ones; tier 2 is the show barn, used only for whatever shortfall tier 1 leaves. Built by partitioning the *already-computed* eligible pool (one `eligibleNpcHorsesForClass` batch call, unchanged from slice 0025's own optimisation) rather than querying per tier, so this adds no extra round trips to the tick's dominant cost. **One consequence worth flagging: a stable that is neither a breeding stable nor the show barn (the Consignment Yard) no longer pads any class's field at all** - the slice's own two-tier definition has no third bucket, and a horse actively listed for sale reasonably shouldn't be competing for the children's ribbons anyway, but this is a real behaviour change from before this stage, not just an internal refactor.
- **Test fixture fallout: every `node:sqlite` harness test that hardcodes an NPC-stable-count-derived player-stable id had to move up by one**, the same maintenance slice 0023's `Dressur Stables` migration required (CLAUDE.md's own note on that session bumping every fixture). Nine files (`test/npc/buying-d1.test.ts` and everything that copies its numbering comment) - `PLAYER`/`STABLE`/`SELLER`/`BUYER`/`MARE_STABLE`/`STALLION_STABLE` constants of 12/13 became 13/14, with the explanatory comments updated to say why.
- New tests: `test/npc/show-barn-d1.test.ts` rewritten for `topUpShowBarnToPlan` (top-up-to-plan, idempotence, per-`(breed,rank)` shortfall, rank seeding incl. the GW/PF/no-endurance case - **note the "no breed holds `disc:endurance`" claim is about the deployed site's live three-breed state, not a fresh database, which ships all eight breeds enabled and so does seed Arabian's endurance rows**, death-day exactness, the wider age spread) and a new `test/showing/show-barn-fill-d1.test.ts` (the rank freeze under real judging, and both two-tier scenarios - tier 1 alone covers the shortfall, tier 1 exhausted and tier 2 fills the remainder short of target).
- Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `npx vitest run` green (94 files, 888 tests, up from 882). **Not** verified in a browser or against real D1 outside the `node:sqlite` harness.

---

**2026-08-05 — Account pause.** Requested directly, not from a slice document: a self-service (and admin-settable) way to pause one account without touching the world clock. Distinct from the existing world-level `world.paused` (which freezes every account at once, including the tick) and from `accounts.active` (which blocks login entirely) - this is a per-account switch a player can flip on themselves before time away and reverse themselves the moment they're back.

- **`accounts.paused` / `accounts.paused_real_ts`** (migration `0174`, plain `ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN`). `paused_real_ts` is audit-trail only, per CLAUDE.md §5.3 - never read to decide anything, only shown to the operator. `setAccountPaused` (`src/db/accounts.ts`) is the one writer, called identically from the player's own self-service route and from `/admin/accounts`.
- **The gate lives in `router.ts`, one `if` beside the existing `must_change_password` gate**: every path redirects to `/account/pause` for a paused account except that page itself. `/admin` is deliberately exempt - admin is "a visibly different place" (the phrase `withAdminPinNag`'s own comment already uses), not a player action, so an admin who pauses their own account can still administer while their own play is on hold. Nothing else needed a check: pausing doesn't touch turns, money, or ownership, so every route's existing checks are simply never reached.
- **`GET/POST /account/pause`** (`accountPauseRoute`, `src/routes/account.ts`; `renderAccountPausePage`, `src/render/auth.ts`) is the one page always reachable. It renders one of two bodies depending on `account.paused`, both a single button with no confirmation step - fully self-reversible any time, so the destructive-action confirmation pattern (`/admin/reset`'s typed word, the account-delete checkbox) doesn't apply. Reached from a new "Account" link in the player nav (`src/render/layout.ts`) - previously `/account/password` had no nav link at all and was only reachable via the forced-password-change redirect; the pause page now links to it too, closing that gap as a side effect.
- **What actually stops, and what doesn't, per the request:** `chargeUpkeep` (`src/db/upkeep.ts`) now joins `accounts` and charges a paused stable nothing - but still advances `last_upkeep_game_day` to the new game day (unlike the pre-existing NPC exemption, which never advances its marker because NPC stables are permanently exempt), so unpausing doesn't present a lump-sum board bill for the whole paused span. `rollAcuteIncidents` (`src/db/incidents.ts`) now left-joins `accounts` and skips the incident-type loop entirely for a paused account's horse - no new onset is rolled - but still advances `last_incident_check_game_day` for the same backlog reason. Everything else keeps running exactly as it already did, because nothing needed to change for it to: `killDueOldHorses`/`assignLifespansAndNoticeFrailty` don't look at ownership at all, `foalDuePregnancies` and `expireListings`/`judgeDueShowClasses` only ever look at the game clock and each row's own status. `resolveAcuteIncidents` is also untouched on purpose - an incident already open when an account pauses still resolves on its existing schedule; pausing stops new bad luck, not luck already in motion.
- **The admin control** (`/admin/accounts`) is a `Pause`/`Unpause` button beside the existing `Deactivate`/`Reactivate` one, reusing the same `setAccountPaused` the self-service route calls. No last-admin-style guard was needed the way `set_admin`/`delete` need one - unlike deactivating your own account or removing the last admin, an account can never lock itself out of unpausing, since `/account/pause` is exempt from its own gate.
- New tests: `test/accounts/pause-d1.test.ts` (new `node:sqlite` harness, same shape as `test/incidents/incidents-d1.test.ts`/`test/ageing/geld-d1.test.ts`) - `setAccountPaused` round-trips both columns, `chargeUpkeep` charges an active stable and not a paused one while advancing both markers, a paused-then-unpaused stable is billed only for its unpaused days (not the whole paused span), and `rollAcuteIncidents` (forced to a deterministic 100%-onset synthetic incident type, ceiling raised to 1.0 for the test only) rolls nothing for a paused horse while an otherwise-identical active horse gets the incident. Added to `tsconfig.json`'s exclude list alongside the other `node:sqlite` test files, per that file's own header comment on why.
- Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `npx vitest run` green (95 files, 892 tests, up from 888). **Not** verified in a browser against real D1 - nobody has pressed the Pause button on a live `wrangler dev`.

---

**2026-08-05 — Two small operator-reported display fixes: the `/world` NPC row, and show class titles/descriptors.**

- **`/world`'s "Everyone" table and its per-stable page (`src/render/world.ts`) no longer print the literal string "Run by the game" in the Run-by or Money columns.** Run-by now reads `NPC`. Money now always prints `s.balance` - real data that was already flowing through (`WorldStableListRow.balance`, populated in `src/routes/world.ts`) but was being discarded at render time per §6.2.1's original reasoning ("an NPC stable's balance is a one-way counter measuring nothing a player's balance measures"). That reasoning is about how an NPC balance *behaves*, not whether it should be visible - the operator asked to see it, so it's shown now. No route or query change; `s.balance` was already correct.
- **Conformation class titles reordered breed-first → label-first: `"German Warmblood Conformation"` → `"Conformation- German Warmblood"`** (and the young-horse equivalent, `"Yearling German Warmblood Conformation"` → `"Yearling Conformation- German Warmblood"`), in the two places `src/db/shows.ts` builds these names (`createShowIfMissing`'s minting path and `buildShowCatalogue`'s on-demand path - both changed together so a joined-vs-minted class can't show two different titles for the same `class_key`). Cosmetic only, no `class_key`/routing change. Only reaches classes minted from now on; existing rows keep whatever name they were given at mint. The operator had just reset the world when this was requested, so no backfill was asked for or done.
- **The descriptor line under a class title (`classRulesSentence`, `src/render/shows.ts`) dropped the breed name and the "Open to every breed"/"purebreds only"/"purebreds and crosses" phrasing entirely** - the title already says the breed (or that there isn't one), so restating it a second line down was pure redundancy. What's left: an age range written as `"4+ years"` (or `"1-2 years"` when the class has a `max_age_game_days`, e.g. the young-horse bands) instead of the older `"at least 4 years old"`/`"no older than N years"` two-clause form, then `sex_restriction`/`requires_gait` when the class actually carries one (unaffected - still real, narrowing information), then the rank word. `classRulesSentence` dropped its `breedName` parameter; the `breedName` field itself stays on `ShowsIndexNextClass`/`ShowPageClassView` since `eligibilityMessage` elsewhere still needs it.
- Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `npx vitest run` green (95 files, 892 tests, unchanged - no test asserted the old strings). **Not** verified in a browser against real D1.

**2026-08-06 — Walkback: on-demand show entry mints parallel classes again instead of refusing at the per-stable cap.** Requested directly by the operator, who is entering four-plus horses at a time in real play: "keep minting parallel classes. continue joining already minted classes, oldest first, if able." This reverses the deliberate departure slice 0025 stage 4 took from its own slice document (recorded in the CLAUDE.md §10 row for that slice and in this log's 2026-08-05 entry) - that session judged the literal spec would refragment sibling rivalry back into one-entry shows; the operator has now weighed that trade-off directly and asked for the literal §7.5a behaviour back.

- **`idx_show_classes_open_key`** (migration `0165`), a `UNIQUE(class_key, rank) WHERE status = 'scheduled'` partial index, was the actual mechanism enforcing "at most one live class per catalogue row" - not application code. Migration `0175` drops it and replaces it with a plain (non-unique) index on the same columns plus `id`, for the ordering the new lookup needs. Several `show_classes` rows sharing a `(class_key, rank)` while `status = 'scheduled'` is now expected, not a bug.
- **`buildCatalogueStatusForHorse`** (`src/db/shows.ts`) now fetches every live class, groups them by `(class_key, rank)`, and within each group picks the **oldest (lowest id) class this stable still has room in** as the join target; `already_entered` now checks across every class in the group, not just one. When no group member has room (or none exists), the row reports as "would mint," exactly as if the cap had never been hit - `entry_cap_reached` can no longer come out of this path.
- **`requestClassEntry`** replaced its single-row `SELECT ... WHERE class_key = ? AND rank = ?` lookup with one that also filters on `(SELECT COUNT(*) ... WHERE entered_by_stable_id = ?) < max_entries_per_stable` and orders by `id ASC LIMIT 1` - the same "oldest with room" rule, enforced at the actual write. No live match mints a new parallel class via `mintClassForSpec`, which no longer needs to handle a unique-index collision (there is nothing left to collide on) - it now returns `number` instead of `number | null`, and the two-attempt retry loop that existed only to recover from that collision is gone; a single pass always succeeds.
- `checkEligibility` (`src/engines/showing/eligibility.ts`) is untouched - `entry_cap_reached` still exists and is still correct for `/shows/:id`'s browse-a-specific-already-existing-class form and the NPC top-up, which target one concrete class id rather than a catalogue row.
- Test `"once a stable's per-class cap is reached, a further request refuses..."` (`test/showing/rank-and-on-demand-d1.test.ts`) rewritten for the new expectation (fourth horse mints a second class and succeeds); a new test alongside it pins "oldest first" by checking a fifth horse joins that same second class rather than minting a third.
- Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `npx vitest run` green (95 files, 893 tests, up from 892). **Not** verified in a browser against real D1.
- **Same-day operator report: applying migration `0175` from `/admin/migrations` on the live site failed** - `D1_ERROR: no such index: idx_show_classes_open_key: SQLITE_ERROR` on the `DROP INDEX` statement. The deployed database's `d1_migrations` table records `0165` as applied (and its columns/behaviour are visibly live - rank-gated classes work on the site today), so the index this migration expected to drop was, for reasons not tracked down, already absent there; `src/db/reset.ts`'s world reset only ever runs `DELETE FROM`, never touches schema, so a reset isn't the cause. `applyPendingMigrations` (`src/db/migrations.ts`) runs each migration's statements plus its tracking-row insert as one D1 batch, and doesn't record the row on failure - so this migration was never actually marked applied on the live database despite the attempt, and editing it directly (rather than filing a follow-up `0176`) doesn't cross the forward-only rule (CLAUDE.md §8: never edit an *applied* migration). Both statements in `0175` now use `IF EXISTS`/`IF NOT EXISTS`, so "make the index gone, then create the new one" succeeds whether or not the old one was ever really there. Re-verified: `npx vitest run` still green (same counts - the guards are no-ops against a fresh replay, which always has the index to drop).
